In a pilot study of four public AI review systems evaluated in six configurations, concern-level analysis suggests that detection alone does not determine review quality; calibration is often the binding constraint. Systems detect non-trivial fractions of official concerns yet most mark 25--55% of concerns on accepted papers as decisive, where, under our operationalization, no official concern on accepted papers was treated as a decisive blocker. Identical overall verdict accuracy can conceal reject-heavy behavior versus low-recall profiles, and low full-review false decisive rates can partly reflect concern dilution rather than calibrated prioritization. Most systems do not emit a native accept/reject, and inferring it from review tone is method-sensitive, reinforcing the need for concern-level diagnostics that remain stable across inference choices. The contribution is a reusable evaluation framework for auditing which concerns AI reviewers identify, how they weight them, and whether those priorities align with the review rationale that informed the paper's final assessment.

</details>


### [149] [HiPO: Hierarchical Preference Optimization for Adaptive Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20140)
*Darsh Kachroo,Adriana Caraeni,Arjun Prasaath Anbazhagan,Brennan Lagasse,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiPO是一种改进的DPO方法，通过将推理任务分解为多个段落并分别计算损失，在保持DPO计算效率的同时提升了复杂推理任务的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO在处理复杂推理任务时存在局限性，因为它对整个响应进行优化，缺乏对多步骤推理中各个子部分的细粒度反馈。现有方法要么擅长稳定的偏好学习（如DPO变体），要么擅长结构化推理（如多智能体RL框架），但未能结合这两者的互补优势。

Method: HiPO是DPO的扩展，它将响应分解为多个推理段落（查询澄清和上下文、推理步骤、答案），并将损失计算为每个段落的DPO损失的加权和。这种方法实现了段落特定的训练，同时保持了DPO的计算效率和训练稳定性。

Result: 在Math Stack Exchange偏好数据集上使用HiPO和DPO微调的多个7B LLM中，HiPO训练的模型在各种常见数学基准测试中表现优于其他模型，并且通过GPT-4评估显示出更好的组织性、逻辑流程和一致性。

Conclusion: HiPO通过引入分层优化方法，成功地将DPO的稳定偏好学习能力与结构化推理的优势相结合，为复杂推理任务的对齐提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is an effective framework for aligning large language models with human preferences, but it struggles with complex reasoning tasks. DPO optimizes for the likelihood of generating preferred over dispreferred responses in their entirety and lacks the granularity to provide feedback on subsections of many-step solutions typical of reasoning tasks. Existing methods excel at either stable preference learning (e.g., DPO variants like KTO and RSO) or structured reasoning (e.g., ReMA's multi-agent RL framework, Tree of Thoughts), but fail to merge these complementary strengths. We propose HiPO (Hierarchical Preference Optimization), an extension of DPO that separates responses into reasoning segments (query clarification and context, reasoning steps, and answer) and computes loss as a weighted sum of the DPO loss for each segment. Our approach enables segment-specific training while maintaining DPO's computational efficiency and training stability. We demonstrate that for multiple 7B LLMs fine-tuned using HiPO and DPO on the Math Stack Exchange preference dataset, the models trained with HiPO outperform the others on a variety of common math benchmarks and achieve greater organization, logical flow, and consistency as measured by GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [150] [Stateless Decision Memory for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20158)
*Vasundra Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 确定性投影内存（DPM）是一种用于受监管企业决策的存储架构，通过仅追加事件日志和任务条件投影，在保持可重放性、可审计性等系统属性的同时，显著优于基于摘要的内存方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在更复杂的带状态内存架构，但受监管领域（承保、理赔裁决、税务审查）的企业部署仍主要采用检索增强管道。这反映了隐藏需求：受监管部署依赖于四个系统属性（确定性重放、可审计理由、多租户隔离、无状态水平扩展），而带状态架构本质上违反了这些属性。

Method: 提出确定性投影内存（DPM）：仅追加事件日志加上决策时的任务条件投影。DPM在决策时只进行一次LLM调用，而不是N次。该方法保持了无状态特性，同时提供更好的事实精度和推理连贯性。

Result: 在三个内存预算下的十个受监管决策案例中，DPM在宽松预算下与基于摘要的内存相当，在预算受限时显著优于后者：在20倍压缩比下，DPM提高事实精度+0.52（Cohen's h=1.17, p=0.0014）和推理连贯性+0.53（h=1.13, p=0.0034）。DPM在绑定预算时快7-15倍，每次决策只进行一次LLM调用。审计表面显示DPM每次决策记录2次LLM调用，而摘要方法记录83-97次。

Conclusion: 无状态性是解释企业偏好较弱但可重放检索管道的关键负载属性，DPM证明可以在不付出检索决策代价的情况下实现这一属性。提出了TAMS架构选择启发式方法，并分析了带状态内存在企业运营条件下的失败原因。

Abstract: Enterprise deployment of long-horizon decision agents in regulated domains (underwriting, claims adjudication, tax examination) is dominated by retrieval-augmented pipelines despite a decade of increasingly sophisticated stateful memory architectures. We argue this reflects a hidden requirement: regulated deployment is load-bearing on four systems properties (deterministic replay, auditable rationale, multi-tenant isolation, statelessness for horizontal scale), and stateful architectures violate them by construction. We propose Deterministic Projection Memory (DPM): an append-only event log plus one task-conditioned projection at decision time. On ten regulated decisioning cases at three memory budgets, DPM matches summarization-based memory at generous budgets and substantially outperforms it when the budget binds: at a 20x compression ratio, DPM improves factual precision by +0.52 (Cohen's h=1.17, p=0.0014) and reasoning coherence by +0.53 (h=1.13, p=0.0034), paired permutation, n=10. DPM is additionally 7-15x faster at binding budgets, making one LLM call at decision time instead of N. A determinism study of 10 replays per case at temperature zero shows both architectures inherit residual API-level nondeterminism, but the asymmetry is structural: DPM exposes one nondeterministic call; summarization exposes N compounding calls. The audit surface follows the same one-versus-N pattern: DPM logs two LLM calls per decision while summarization logs 83-97 on LongHorizon-Bench. We conclude with TAMS, a practitioner heuristic for architecture selection, and a failure analysis of stateful memory under enterprise operating conditions. The contribution is the argument that statelessness is the load-bearing property explaining enterprise's preference for weaker but replayable retrieval pipelines, and that DPM demonstrates this property is attainable without the decisioning penalty retrieval pays.

</details>


### [151] [Mol-Debate: Multi-Agent Debate Improves Structural Reasoning in Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20254)
*Wengyu Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mol-Debate：一种通过迭代生成-辩论-精炼循环实现动态多视角推理的文本引导分子设计新范式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导分子设计方法（如RAG、CoT提示、微调或RL）主要采用一次性生成流程，强调有限的特定推理视角，而真实药物发现需要动态多视角批判和迭代精炼来协调语义意图与结构可行性之间的冲突。

Method: 提出Mol-Debate生成范式，通过迭代生成-辩论-精炼循环实现动态推理。采用面向视角的编排机制解决关键挑战：开发者-辩论者冲突、全局-局部结构推理、静态-动态集成。

Result: 在ChEBI-20上达到59.82%精确匹配率，在S^2-Bench上达到50.52%加权成功率，优于现有通用和化学基线方法，实现最先进性能。

Conclusion: Mol-Debate通过模拟真实药物发现中的动态多视角批判过程，有效解决了文本引导分子设计中语义意图与结构可行性的协调问题，为AI驱动的药物发现提供了更符合实际需求的生成范式。

Abstract: Text-guided molecular design is a key capability for AI-driven drug discovery, yet it remains challenging to map sequential natural-language instructions with non-linear molecular structures under strict chemical constraints. Most existing approaches, including RAG, CoT prompting, and fine-tuning or RL, emphasize a small set of ad-hoc reasoning perspectives implemented in a largely one-shot generation pipeline. In contrast, real-world drug discovery relies on dynamic, multi-perspective critique and iterative refinement to reconcile semantic intent with structural feasibility. Motivated by this, we propose Mol-Debate, a generation paradigm that enables such dynamic reasoning through an iterative generate-debate-refine loop. We further characterize key challenges in this paradigm and address them through perspective-oriented orchestration, including developer-debater conflict, global-local structural reasoning, and static-dynamic integration. Experiments demonstrate that Mol-Debate achieves state-of-the-art performance against strong general and chemical baselines, reaching 59.82% exact match on ChEBI-20 and 50.52% weighted success rate on S$^2$-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/wyuzh/Mol-Debate.

</details>


### [152] [Memory-Augmented LLM-based Multi-Agent System for Automated Feature Generation on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20261)
*Fengxian Dong,Zhi Zheng,Xiao Han,Wei Chen,Jingqing Ruan,Tong Xu,Yong Chen,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MALMAS：一种基于记忆增强LLM的多智能体系统，用于自动化特征生成，通过分解生成过程、路由机制和记忆模块实现迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化特征生成方法依赖预定义算子库，无法利用任务语义，限制了生成特征的多样性和价值。现有LLM方法虽然引入了语义信号，但仍受限于固定生成模式和缺乏学习目标反馈。

Method: 提出MALMAS系统：1）多智能体架构，将生成过程分解为不同职责的智能体；2）路由智能体根据迭代激活合适的智能体子集；3）集成包含过程记忆、反馈记忆和概念记忆的记忆模块，支持迭代优化。

Result: 在多个公共数据集上与最先进基线方法的广泛实验证明了该方法的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MALMAS通过多智能体协作和记忆增强机制，有效解决了现有自动化特征生成方法的局限性，能够生成更高质量和多样性的特征。

Abstract: Automated feature generation extracts informative features from raw tabular data without manual intervention and is crucial for accurate, generalizable machine learning. Traditional methods rely on predefined operator libraries and cannot leverage task semantics, limiting their ability to produce diverse, high-value features for complex tasks. Recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches introduce richer semantic signals, but still suffer from a restricted feature space due to fixed generation patterns and from the absence of feedback from the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a Memory-Augmented LLM-based Multi-Agent System (\textbf{MALMAS}) for automated feature generation. MALMAS decomposes the generation process into agents with distinct responsibilities, and a Router Agent activates an appropriate subset of agents per iteration, further broadening exploration of the feature space. We further integrate a memory module comprising procedural memory, feedback memory, and conceptual memory, enabling iterative refinement that adaptively guides subsequent feature generation and improves feature quality and diversity. Extensive experiments on multiple public datasets against state-of-the-art baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. The code is available at https://github.com/fxdong24/MALMAS

</details>


### [153] [FSFM: A Biologically-Inspired Framework for Selective Forgetting of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20300)
*Yingjie Gu,Bo Xiong,Yijuan Guo,Chao Li,Xiaojing Zhang,Liqiang Wang,Pengcheng Ren,Qi Sun,Jingyao Ma,Shidang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM智能体中的选择性遗忘机制，受人类认知过程启发，通过智能记忆修剪、动态更新过时内容、主动遗忘恶意输入来提升效率、质量和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的内存管理对效率、质量和安全性至关重要。现有研究多关注记忆保留，而受人类认知过程（海马索引/巩固理论和艾宾浩斯遗忘曲线）启发的选择性遗忘机制尚未充分探索。作者认为在资源受限环境中，精心设计的遗忘机制与记忆同等重要，能在效率、质量和安全三个维度带来显著收益。

Method: 建立遗忘机制的分类学：被动衰减型、主动删除型、安全触发型和自适应强化型。基于LLM智能体架构和向量数据库的最新进展，提出详细规范、实施策略，并通过受控实验进行实证验证。

Result: 实验结果显示显著改进：访问效率提升8.49%，内容质量（信噪比）提升29.2%，安全性能实现100%安全风险消除。该框架成功将认知神经科学原理应用于AI系统。

Conclusion: 选择性遗忘是下一代LLM智能体在现实世界资源受限场景中运行的基本能力。该研究连接了认知神经科学与AI系统，为实际部署提供实用解决方案，同时解决伦理和法规合规问题。贡献与AI原生内存系统和负责任AI发展相一致，并指出了未来挑战和发展方向。

Abstract: For LLM agents, memory management critically impacts efficiency, quality, and security. While much research focuses on retention, selective forgetting--inspired by human cognitive processes (hippocampal indexing/consolidation theory and Ebbinghaus forgetting curve)--remains underexplored. We argue that in resource-constrained environments, a well-designed forgetting mechanism is as crucial as remembering, delivering benefits across three dimensions: (1) efficiency via intelligent memory pruning, (2) quality by dynamically updating outdated preferences and context, and (3) security through active forgetting of malicious inputs, sensitive data, and privacy-compromising content. Our framework establishes a taxonomy of forgetting mechanisms: passive decay-based, active deletion-based, safety-triggered, and adaptive reinforcement-based. Building on advances in LLM agent architectures and vector databases, we present detailed specifications, implementation strategies, and empirical validation from controlled experiments. Results show significant improvements: access efficiency (+8.49%), content quality (+29.2% signal-to-noise ratio), and security performance (100% elimination of security risks). Our work bridges cognitive neuroscience and AI systems, offering practical solutions for real-world deployment while addressing ethical and regulatory compliance. The paper concludes with challenges and future directions, establishing selective forgetting as a fundamental capability for next-generation LLM agents operating in real-world, resource-constrained scenarios. Our contributions align with AI-native memory systems and responsible AI development.

</details>


### [154] [Self-Awareness before Action: Mitigating Logical Inertia via Proactive Cognitive Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20413)
*Fulong Fan,Peilin Liu,Fengzhe Liu,Shuyan Yang,Gang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SABA是一个推理框架，通过引入对缺失前提的自我意识来解决LLMs在非交互式谜题中因早期假设错误而导致的推理不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在许多推理任务上表现良好，但往往缺乏对当前知识或推理状态是否完整的意识。在非交互式谜题设置中，叙事固定且底层结构隐藏，模型在前提不完整的情况下形成早期假设后，会将错误传播到整个推理过程，导致结论不稳定。

Method: SABA将推理制定为结构化状态构建和障碍解决的递归过程：首先应用信息融合将叙事整合为可验证的基础状态，然后使用查询驱动的结构化推理来识别和解决缺失或未明确的前提，将其转化为查询，通过假设构建和状态细化逐步完善推理状态。

Result: SABA在非交互式侦探谜题基准测试的所有三个难度划分上都取得了最佳性能，并在多个公共基准测试中保持领先结果。

Conclusion: SABA通过引入对缺失前提的自我意识，有效解决了LLMs在非交互式推理任务中的早期假设错误传播问题，显著提升了推理稳定性和准确性。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on many reasoning tasks, yet they often lack awareness of whether their current knowledge or reasoning state is complete. In non-interactive puzzle settings, the narrative is fixed and the underlying structure is hidden; once a model forms an early hypothesis under incomplete premises, it can propagate that error throughout the reasoning process, leading to unstable conclusions. To address this issue, we propose SABA, a reasoning framework that explicitly introduces self-awareness of missing premises before making the final decision. SABA formulates reasoning as a recursive process that alternates between structured state construction and obstacle resolution: it first applies Information Fusion to consolidate the narrative into a verifiable base state, and then uses Query-driven Structured Reasoning to identify and resolve missing or underspecified premises by turning them into queries and progressively completing the reasoning state through hypothesis construction and state refinement. Across multiple evaluation metrics, SABA achieves the best performance on all three difficulty splits of the non-interactive Detective Puzzle benchmark, and it also maintains leading results on multiple public benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [MedSkillAudit: A Domain-Specific Audit Framework for Medical Research Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20441)
*Yingyong Hou,Xinyuan Lao,Huimei Wang,Qianyu Yao,Wei Chen,Bocheng Huang,Fei Sun,Yuxian Lv,Weiqi Lei,Xueqian Wen,Pengfei Xia,Zhujun Tan,Shengyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发并初步评估了针对医学研究智能体技能的领域特定审计框架MedSkillAudit，在可靠性方面与专家评审达成中等一致性，优于人类评审员间的一致性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学研究智能体技能需要超越通用评估的安全保障，包括科学完整性、方法有效性、可重复性和边界安全性。需要开发针对医学研究领域的特定审计框架来确保技能部署前的质量。

Method: 开发了MedSkillAudit（skill-auditor@1.0）分层框架，评估技能部署前的准备状态。评估了75个技能（5个医学研究类别，每类15个）。两位专家独立评估质量分数（0-100）、发布处置等级（生产就绪/有限发布/仅测试版/拒绝）和高风险失败标志。使用ICC(2,1)和线性加权Cohen's kappa量化系统-专家一致性，并与人类评审员间基线进行对比。

Result: 平均共识质量分数为72.4（SD=13.0）；57.3%的技能低于有限发布阈值。MedSkillAudit的ICC(2,1)=0.449（95% CI: 0.250-0.610），优于人类评审员间的ICC=0.300。系统-共识分数差异（SD=9.5）小于专家间差异（SD=12.4），无方向性偏差（Wilcoxon p=0.613）。协议设计类别一致性最强（ICC=0.551）；学术写作类别ICC为负值（-0.567），反映结构性评估标准与专家判断不匹配。

Conclusion: 领域特定的部署前审计可为医学研究智能体技能治理提供实用基础，通过针对科学用例的结构化审计工作流程补充通用质量检查。该方法在可靠性方面表现出与专家评审的中等一致性，优于人类评审员间的一致性水平。

Abstract: Background: Agent skills are increasingly deployed as modular, reusable capability units in AI agent systems. Medical research agent skills require safeguards beyond general-purpose evaluation, including scientific integrity, methodological validity, reproducibility, and boundary safety. This study developed and preliminarily evaluated a domain-specific audit framework for medical research agent skills, with a focus on reliability against expert review. Methods: We developed MedSkillAudit (skill-auditor@1.0), a layered framework assessing skill release readiness before deployment. We evaluated 75 skills across five medical research categories (15 per category). Two experts independently assigned a quality score (0-100), an ordinal release disposition (Production Ready / Limited Release / Beta Only / Reject), and a high-risk failure flag. System-expert agreement was quantified using ICC(2,1) and linearly weighted Cohen's kappa, benchmarked against the human inter-rater baseline. Results: The mean consensus quality score was 72.4 (SD = 13.0); 57.3% of skills fell below the Limited Release threshold. MedSkillAudit achieved ICC(2,1) = 0.449 (95% CI: 0.250-0.610), exceeding the human inter-rater ICC of 0.300. System-consensus score divergence (SD = 9.5) was smaller than inter-expert divergence (SD = 12.4), with no directional bias (Wilcoxon p = 0.613). Protocol Design showed the strongest category-level agreement (ICC = 0.551); Academic Writing showed a negative ICC (-0.567), reflecting a structural rubric-expert mismatch. Conclusions: Domain-specific pre-deployment audit may provide a practical foundation for governing medical research agent skills, complementing general-purpose quality checks with structured audit workflows tailored to scientific use cases.

</details>


### [156] [Measuring the Machine: Evaluating Generative AI as Pluralist Sociotechical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20545)
*Rebecca L. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MaSH Loops框架，将生成式AI评估重构为描述性、过程导向的多元社会技术系统分析，强调评估本身是构成性干预而非中立测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI评估基准存在局限性：功能主义基准将模型视为孤立预测器，规范性方法评估系统"应该是什么"，两者都掩盖了意义和价值在社会技术过程中的构建方式，在多元文化背景下可能固化狭隘的文化视角。

Method: 提出Machine-Society-Human (MaSH) Loops框架，追踪模型、用户和制度如何递归地共同构建意义和价值；开发World Values Benchmark，基于世界价值观调查数据，采用结构化提示集和锚感知评分；通过两个实证案例验证：早期GPT-3的价值漂移和房地产领域的社会技术评估。

Result: 概念上：将评估重构为递归的、具身的过程；方法上：建立了基于分布方法的价值观基准；实证上：展示了MaSH Loops在分析价值漂移和社会技术评估中的应用价值。

Conclusion: 静态基准对生成式AI评估不足，负责任评估需要多元、过程导向的框架，使价值构建过程可见；评估本身是治理场所，塑造AI系统的理解、部署和信任。

Abstract: In measurement theory, instruments do not simply record reality; they help constitute what is observed. The same holds for generative AI evaluation: benchmarks do not just measure, they shape what models appear to be. Functionalist benchmarks treat models as isolated predictors, while prescriptive approaches assess what systems ought to be. Both obscure the sociotechnical processes through which meaning and values are enacted, risking the reification of narrow cultural perspectives in pluralist contexts.
  This thesis advances a descriptive alternative. It argues that generative AI must be evaluated as a pluralist sociotechnical system and develops Machine-Society-Human (MaSH) Loops, a framework for tracing how models, users, and institutions recursively co-construct meaning and values. Evaluation shifts from judging outputs to examining how values are enacted in interaction.
  Three contributions follow. Conceptually, MaSH Loops reframes evaluation as recursive, enactive process. Methodologically, the World Values Benchmark introduces a distributional approach grounded in World Values Survey data, structured prompt sets, and anchor-aware scoring. Empirically, the thesis demonstrates these through two cases: value drift in early GPT-3 and sociotechnical evaluation in real estate. A final chapter draws on participatory realism to argue that prompting and evaluation are constitutive interventions, not neutral observations.
  The thesis argues that static benchmarks are insufficient for generative AI. Responsible evaluation requires pluralist, process-oriented frameworks that make visible whose values are enacted. Evaluation is therefore a site of governance, shaping how AI systems are understood, deployed, and trusted.

</details>


### [157] [Self-Guided Plan Extraction for Instruction-Following Tasks with Goal-Conditional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20601)
*Zoya Volovikova,Nikita Sorokin,Dmitriy Lukashevskiy,Aleksandr Panov,Alexey Skrynnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SuperIgor框架通过自学习机制让语言模型生成和优化高级计划，减少人工标注需求，在动态随机环境中实现更好的指令跟随和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预定义子任务，需要大量人工数据集标注。SuperIgor旨在通过自学习机制减少人工标注需求，让语言模型能够自主生成和优化高级计划。

Method: 采用迭代协同训练：RL智能体训练以遵循生成的计划，语言模型根据RL反馈和偏好调整修改计划。形成智能体与规划器共同改进的反馈循环。

Result: 在动态丰富且随机的环境中验证，SuperIgor智能体比基线方法更严格地遵循指令，同时对未见过的指令表现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SuperIgor框架通过自学习机制和协同训练，实现了更严格的指令跟随和更好的泛化性能，为减少人工标注需求的指令跟随任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce SuperIgor, a framework for instruction-following tasks. Unlike prior methods that rely on predefined subtasks, SuperIgor enables a language model to generate and refine high-level plans through a self-learning mechanism, reducing the need for manual dataset annotation. Our approach involves iterative co-training: an RL agent is trained to follow the generated plans, while the language model adapts and modifies these plans based on RL feedback and preferences. This creates a feedback loop where both the agent and the planner improve jointly. We validate our framework in environments with rich dynamics and stochasticity. Results show that SuperIgor agents adhere to instructions more strictly than baseline methods, while also demonstrating strong generalization to previously unseen instructions.

</details>


### [158] [pAI/MSc: ML Theory Research with Humans on the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20622)
*Mahmoud Abdelmoneum,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: pAI/MSc是一个开源、可定制、模块化的多智能体系统，旨在大幅减少将指定假设转化为文献基础扎实、数学严谨、实验支持、适合投稿的论文草稿所需的人工指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术研究流程中，将假设转化为完整的论文草稿需要大量人工指导和繁琐工作。作者旨在开发一个实用工具，显著减少这一过程中的人工干预，提高研究效率。

Method: 开发了一个开源、可定制、模块化的多智能体系统，专注于机器学习理论和相关定量领域。系统采用多智能体架构，各模块分工协作处理文献调研、数学推导、实验设计和论文撰写等任务。

Result: 提出了pAI/MSc系统框架，该系统能够将指定假设转化为具有文献基础、数学严谨性、实验支持和投稿导向的论文草稿，大幅减少了所需的人工指导。

Conclusion: pAI/MSc为学术研究流程提供了一个实用的多智能体解决方案，专注于减少将假设转化为完整论文草稿的人工工作量，特别适用于机器学习理论和相关定量领域。

Abstract: We present pAI/MSc, an open-source, customizable, modular multi-agent system for academic research workflows. Our goal is not autonomous scientific ideation, nor fully automated research. It is narrower and more practical: to reduce by orders of magnitude the human steering required to turn a specified hypothesis into a literature-grounded, mathematically established, experimentally supported, submission-oriented manuscript draft. pAI/MSc is built with a current emphasis on machine learning theory and adjacent quantitative fields.

</details>


### [159] [CHORUS: An Agentic Framework for Generating Realistic Deliberation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20651)
*A. Koursaris,G. Domalis,A. Apostolopoulou,K. Kanaris,D. Tsakalidis,I. E. Livieris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chorus是一个基于LLM的智能体框架，通过具有行为一致性的角色生成逼真的在线讨论数据，解决了大规模审议数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台由于访问限制、伦理问题和数据质量不一致，缺乏大规模审议数据，这限制了对在线话语动态的深入理解。

Method: Chorus框架使用具有行为一致性的LLM驱动角色，每个角色由具有讨论记忆的自主智能体控制，参与时间基于泊松过程的时间模型，模拟真实用户的异质参与模式，并支持结构化工具使用以访问外部资源。

Result: 框架在Deliberate平台上部署，由30位专家从内容真实性、讨论连贯性和分析实用性三个维度评估，确认Chorus是生成高质量审议数据的实用工具。

Conclusion: Chorus框架能够生成适合在线话语分析的高质量审议数据，为解决大规模审议数据稀缺问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding the intricate dynamics of online discourse depends on large-scale deliberation data, a resource that remains scarce across interactive web platforms due to restrictive accessibility policies, ethical concerns and inconsistent data quality. In this paper, we propose Chorus, an agentic framework, which orchestrates LLM-powered actors with behaviorally consistent personas to generate realistic deliberation discussions. Each actor is governed by an autonomous agent equipped with memory of the evolving discussion, while participation timing is governed by a principled Poisson process-based temporal model, which approximates the heterogeneous engagement patterns of real users. The framework is further supported by structured tool usage, enabling actors to access external resources and facilitating integration with interactive web platforms. The framework was deployed on the \textsc{Deliberate} platform and evaluated by 30 expert participants across three dimensions: content realism, discussion coherence and analytical utility, confirming Chorus as a practical tool for generating high-quality deliberation data suitable for online discourse analysis

</details>


### [160] [Learning to Evolve: A Self-Improving Framework for Multi-Agent Systems via Textual Parameter Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20714)
*Shan He,Runze Wang,Zhuoyun Du,Huiyu Bai,Zouying Cao,Yu Cheng,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TPGO框架通过将多智能体系统建模为文本参数图，引入文本梯度和基于元学习的优化策略，实现多智能体系统的自我进化与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统设计优化过程复杂且劳动密集，现有自动优化方法主要关注平面提示调优，缺乏对MAS复杂交互结构的理解，且优化器是静态的，无法从经验中学习改进优化策略。

Method: 1. 将MAS建模为文本参数图（TPG），将智能体、工具和工作流作为模块化、可优化的节点；2. 从执行轨迹中推导"文本梯度"（结构化自然语言反馈），精确定位故障并建议细粒度修改；3. 提出组相对智能体优化（GRAO），一种基于元学习的策略，通过分析历史优化经验学习如何更有效地提出更新。

Result: 在GAIA和MCP-Universe等复杂基准测试上的广泛实验表明，TPGO显著提升了最先进智能体框架的性能，通过自动化、自我改进的优化实现了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: TPGO框架通过结构化建模、文本梯度反馈和元学习优化策略，解决了多智能体系统优化中的结构意识和经验学习问题，实现了系统的自我进化能力。

Abstract: Designing and optimizing multi-agent systems (MAS) is a complex, labor-intensive process of "Agent Engineering." Existing automatic optimization methods, primarily focused on flat prompt tuning, lack the structural awareness to debug the intricate web of interactions in MAS. More critically, these optimizers are static; they do not learn from experience to improve their own optimization strategies. To address these gaps, we introduce Textual Parameter Graph Optimization (TPGO), a framework that enables a multi-agent system to learn to evolve. TPGO first models the MAS as a Textual Parameter Graph (TPG), where agents, tools, and workflows are modular, optimizable nodes. To guide evolution, we derive "textual gradients," structured natural language feedback from execution traces, to pinpoint failures and suggest granular modifications. The core of our framework is Group Relative Agent Optimization (GRAO), a novel meta-learning strategy that learns from historical optimization experiences. By analyzing past successes and failures, GRAO becomes progressively better at proposing effective updates, allowing the system to learn how to optimize itself. Extensive experiments on complex benchmarks like GAIA and MCP-Universe show that TPGO significantly enhances the performance of state-of-the-art agent frameworks, achieving higher success rates through automated, self-improving optimization.

</details>


### [161] [Interval POMDP Shielding for Imperfect-Perception Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20728)
*William Scarbro,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于置信区间的运行时防护盾方法，通过有限标注数据估计感知不确定性，为自主系统提供有限时域安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统依赖学习感知可能因传感器读数误分类而做出不安全决策，需要防护机制来阻止可能违反安全的动作。

Method: 从有限标注数据构建感知结果概率的置信区间，将系统建模为有限区间部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，提出算法计算与观测一致的保守信念集，构建运行时防护盾。

Result: 防护盾提供有限时域保证：若真实感知不确定性率在学习的区间内，则防护盾允许的每个动作都满足安全下界。四个案例研究表明该方法优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 基于置信区间的防护盾方法能有效提升自主系统的安全性，通过有限数据估计不确定性并提供概率安全保证。

Abstract: Autonomous systems that rely on learned perception can make unsafe decisions when sensor readings are misclassified. We study shielding for this setting: given a proposed action, a shield blocks actions that could violate safety. We consider the common case where system dynamics are known but perception uncertainty must be estimated from finite labeled data. From these data we build confidence intervals for the probabilities of perception outcomes and use them to model the system as a finite Interval Partially Observable Markov Decision Process with discrete states and actions. We then propose an algorithm to compute a conservative set of beliefs over the underlying state that is consistent with the observations seen so far.
  This enables us to construct a runtime shield that comes with a finite-horizon guarantee: with high probability over the training data, if the true perception uncertainty rates lie within the learned intervals, then every action admitted by the shield satisfies a stated lower bound on safety. Experiments on four case studies show that our shielding approach (and variants derived from it) improves the safety of the system over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [162] [Where and What: Reasoning Dynamic and Implicit Preferences in Situated Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20749)
*Dongding Lin,Jian Wang,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SiPeR框架，通过场景转换估计和贝叶斯逆推理解决情境对话推荐中的动态偏好理解问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境对话推荐需要理解动态和隐式的用户偏好，因为周围场景会影响用户兴趣且两者可能在对话中演变，这种复杂性显著影响推荐的时机和相关性

Method: 提出SiPeR框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 场景转换估计，评估当前场景是否满足用户需求并在必要时引导用户到更合适场景；2) 贝叶斯逆推理，利用多模态大语言模型的似然度预测用户在场景内对候选物品的偏好

Result: 在两个代表性基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SiPeR在推荐准确性和响应生成质量方面均表现出优越性

Conclusion: SiPeR框架通过整合场景转换估计和贝叶斯逆推理，有效解决了情境对话推荐中的动态偏好理解问题，提高了推荐的准确性和相关性

Abstract: Situated conversational recommendation (SCR), which utilizes visual scenes grounded in specific environments and natural language dialogue to deliver contextually appropriate recommendations, has emerged as a promising research direction due to its close alignment with real-world scenarios. Compared to traditional recommendations, SCR requires a deeper understanding of dynamic and implicit user preferences, as the surrounding scene often influences users' underlying interests, while both may evolve across conversations. This complexity significantly impacts the timing and relevance of recommendations. To address this, we propose situated preference reasoning (SiPeR), a novel framework that integrates two core mechanisms: (1) Scene transition estimation, which estimates whether the current scene satisfies user needs, and guides the user toward a more suitable scene when necessary; and (2) Bayesian inverse inference, which leverages the likelihood of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to predict user preferences about candidate items within the scene. Extensive experiments on two representative benchmarks demonstrate SiPeR's superiority in both recommendation accuracy and response generation quality. The code and data are available at https://github.com/DongdingLin/SiPeR.

</details>


### [163] [V-tableR1: Process-Supervised Multimodal Table Reasoning with Critic-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20755)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: V-tableR1是一个过程监督强化学习框架，通过表格的确定性网格结构作为视觉测试平台，引导多模态大语言模型进行可验证的推理，从黑箱模式匹配转向可验证的逻辑推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs仅基于最终结果训练，将视觉推理视为黑箱，依赖表面模式匹配而非严谨的多步推理。虽然可验证奖励的强化学习可以强制执行透明推理轨迹，但将其扩展到视觉领域面临将抽象逻辑锚定到连续像素空间的模糊性挑战。

Method: 利用表格的确定性网格结构作为理想视觉测试平台。使用专门的批评者VLM对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的步骤级反馈。提出Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO)算法，整合过程奖励、解耦策略约束和长度感知动态采样。

Result: V-tableR1明确惩罚视觉幻觉和捷径猜测。在复杂表格基准测试中，V-tableR1 4B在开源模型中达到最先进精度，超越规模达18倍的模型，并显著优于其SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态推理从黑箱模式匹配转变为可验证的逻辑推导，V-tableR1为视觉推理提供了可验证的严谨推理框架，解决了视觉领域强化学习的模糊性问题。

Abstract: We introduce V-tableR1, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework that elicits rigorous, verifiable reasoning from multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Current MLLMs trained solely on final outcomes often treat visual reasoning as a black box, relying on superficial pattern matching rather than performing rigorous multi-step inference. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards could enforce transparent reasoning trajectories, extending it to visual domains remains severely hindered by the ambiguity of grounding abstract logic into continuous pixel space. We solve this by leveraging the deterministic grid structure of tables as an ideal visual testbed. V-tableR1 employs a specialized critic VLM to provide dense, step-level feedback on the explicit visual chain-of-thought generated by a policy VLM. To optimize this system, we propose Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO), a novel RL algorithm integrating process rewards, decoupled policy constraints, and length-aware dynamic sampling. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that V-tableR1 explicitly penalizes visual hallucinations and shortcut guessing. By fundamentally shifting multimodal inference from black-box pattern matching to verifiable logical derivation, V-tableR1 4B establishes state-of-the-art accuracy among open-source models on complex tabular benchmarks, outperforming models up to 18x its size and improving over its SFT baseline

</details>


### [164] [Diagnosing CFG Interpretation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20811)
*Hanqi Li,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在解释上下文无关文法时表现出层次性退化：能保持表面语法但难以维持结构语义，在深度递归和高分支下性能崩溃，且依赖关键词语义引导而非纯符号归纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs越来越多地集成到智能体系统中，它们需要遵循动态定义的机器可解释接口。需要评估LLMs作为上下文解释器的能力：给定新的上下文无关文法时，能否生成语法有效、行为功能正常且语义忠实的输出。

Method: 引入RoboGrid框架，通过控制递归深度、表达式复杂性和表面风格的应力测试，解耦语法、行为和语义。实验包括CoT推理测试，并引入"Alien"词典来检验语义引导依赖。

Result: 发现一致的层次性退化：LLMs通常能保持表面语法但难以维持结构语义。CoT推理提供部分缓解，但在结构密度（特别是深度递归和高分支）下性能崩溃，极端深度时语义对齐消失。"Alien"词典实验显示LLMs依赖关键词的语义引导而非纯符号归纳。

Conclusion: 这些发现指出了可靠、文法无关的智能体所需的层次状态跟踪存在关键差距，LLMs在解释动态定义的机器接口时存在结构性限制。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly integrated into agentic systems, they must adhere to dynamically defined, machine-interpretable interfaces. We evaluate LLMs as in-context interpreters: given a novel context-free grammar, can LLMs generate syntactically valid, behaviorally functional, and semantically faithful outputs? We introduce RoboGrid, a framework that disentangles syntax, behavior, and semantics through controlled stress-tests of recursion depth, expression complexity, and surface styles. Our experiments reveal a consistent hierarchical degradation: LLMs often maintain surface syntax but fail to preserve structural semantics. Despite the partial mitigation provided by CoT reasoning, performance collapses under structural density, specifically deep recursion and high branching, with semantic alignment vanishing at extreme depths. Furthermore, "Alien" lexicons reveal that LLMs rely on semantic bootstrapping from keywords rather than pure symbolic induction. These findings pinpoint critical gaps in hierarchical state-tracking required for reliable, grammar-agnostic agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [SL(C)AMma: Simultaneous Localisation, (Calibration) and Mapping With a Magnetometer Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19946)
*Thomas Edridge,Manon Kok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种基于磁力计阵列的SLAM滤波算法：SLAMma用于磁定位与建图，SLCAMma联合估计磁力计标定参数，显著降低漂移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内定位面临GNSS信号衰减和惯性传感器积分漂移问题。虽然磁场的SLAM可通过回环减少漂移，但探索新区域仍具挑战。磁力计阵列可直接估计里程计，但传感器间测量不一致性影响里程计估计和回环检测

Method: 提出两种滤波算法：1) SLAMma：基于磁力计阵列的磁场SLAM；2) SLCAMma：扩展SLAMma以联合估计磁力计标定参数。通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证标定参数估计精度，实验验证算法性能

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟显示，在足够方向激励下可准确估计标定参数，磁力计可实现传感器间测量一致性。10个数据集的实验验证表明，在单磁力计SLAM失败的情况下，SLAMma和SLCAMma能提供良好轨迹估计，相比惯性传感器积分减少80%以上的漂移

Conclusion: 提出的SLAMma和SLCAMma算法有效解决了磁力计阵列测量不一致性问题，显著提高了室内定位精度，特别是在单磁力计SLAM失败的情况下仍能提供可靠的轨迹估计

Abstract: Indoor localisation techniques suffer from attenuated Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and from the accumulation of unbounded drift by integration of proprioceptive sensors. Magnetic field-based Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) reduces drift through loop closures by revisiting previously seen locations, but extended exploration of unseen areas remains challenging. Recently, magnetometer arrays have demonstrated significant benefits over single magnetometers, as they can directly estimate the odometry. However, inconsistencies between magnetometer measurements negatively affect odometry estimates and complicate loop closure detection. We propose two filtering algorithms: The first focuses on magnetic field-based SLAM using a magnetometer array (SLAMma). The second extends this to jointly estimate the magnetometer calibration parameters (SLCAMma). We demonstrate, using Monte Carlo simulations, that the calibration parameters can be accurately estimated when there is sufficient orientation excitation, and that magnetometers achieve inter-sensor measurement consistency regardless of the type of motion. Experimental validation on ten datasets confirms these results, and we demonstrate that in cases where single magnetometer SLAM fails, SLAMma and SLCAMma provide good trajectory estimates with, more than 80% drift reduction compared to integration of proprioceptive sensors.

</details>


### [166] [Radar Odometry Subject to High Tilt Dynamics of Subarctic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19962)
*Matěj Boxan,William Larivée-Hardy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对传统旋转FMCW雷达里程计在平坦地面假设下的局限性，提出了一种新型雷达-惯性里程计方法，特别适用于具有高动态地形特征的亚北极环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转FMCW雷达里程计方法通常假设平坦地面条件，这在城市环境或平坦矿区足够适用，但在亚北极环境的高度动态地形中，标准特征提取和状态估计技术面临挑战。需要开发能够处理大俯仰角（高达30°）和滚转角（高达8°）变化的方法。

Method: 提出了一种新型雷达-惯性里程计方法，采用倾斜接近子图搜索技术，并在扫描点与估计旋转轴之间的垂直位移上设置硬阈值。该方法专门设计用于处理高动态地形条件。

Result: 在具有挑战性的条件下（连续扫描间俯仰角差异达13°，滚转角差异达4°），新方法在2公里长的动态轨迹上比第二好的比较方法提升了0.3%的性能。在城市基准测试中表现出最先进的性能，并在具有高横向滑移和陡峭沟渠穿越的复杂雷达序列中进行了性能分析。

Conclusion: 提出的雷达-惯性里程计方法通过倾斜接近子图搜索和垂直位移硬阈值技术，有效解决了传统方法在高度动态地形中的局限性，在亚北极等挑战性环境中实现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Rotating FMCW radar odometry methods often assume flat ground conditions. While this assumption is sufficient in many scenarios, including urban environments or flat mining setups, the highly dynamic terrain of subarctic environments poses a challenge to standard feature extraction and state estimation techniques. This paper benchmarks three existing radar odometry methods under demanding conditions, exhibiting up to 13° in pitch and 4° in roll difference between consecutive scans, with absolute pitch and roll reaching 30° and 8°, respectively. Furthermore, we propose a novel radar-inertial odometry method utilizing tilt-proximity submap search and a hard threshold for vertical displacement between scan points and the estimated axis of rotation. Experimental results demonstrate a state-of-the-art performance of our method on an urban baseline and a 0.3% improvement over the second-best comparative method on a 2-kilometer-long dynamic trajectory. Finally, we analyze the performance of the four evaluated methods on a complex radar sequence characterized by high lateral slip and a steep ditch traversal.

</details>


### [167] [Efficient Reinforcement Learning using Linear Koopman Dynamics for Nonlinear Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19980)
*Wenjian Hao,Yuxuan Fang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的强化学习框架，通过Koopman算子学习线性提升动力学，结合演员-评论家架构优化参数化闭环控制器，使用单步预测而非多步传播降低计算成本，在仿真和硬件平台上验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性机器人系统的最优闭环控制问题，传统模型自由强化学习方法样本效率低，而基于精确系统动力学的经典模型方法在实际应用中难以获得准确模型。需要开发既能利用模型优势又不需要精确先验知识的强化学习框架。

Method: 基于Koopman算子理论学习线性提升动力学模型，将其集成到演员-评论家架构中进行策略优化。采用单步预测而非多步传播来估计策略梯度，减少计算成本并缓解模型滚动误差。构建在线小批量策略梯度框架，能够从流式交互数据中持续改进策略。

Result: 在多个模拟非线性控制基准测试以及Kinova Gen3机械臂和Unitree Go1四足机器人两个真实硬件平台上进行实验验证。结果显示：相比模型自由强化学习基线具有更好的样本效率；相比基于模型的强化学习基线具有更优的控制性能；与依赖精确系统动力学的经典模型方法控制性能相当。

Conclusion: 提出的基于Koopman算子的模型强化学习框架有效解决了非线性机器人系统的闭环控制问题，在保持计算效率的同时实现了高性能控制，为实际机器人应用提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimal closed-loop control of nonlinear robotic systems. The proposed approach learns linear lifted dynamics through Koopman operator theory and integrates the resulting model into an actor-critic architecture for policy optimization, where the policy represents a parameterized closed-loop controller. To reduce computational cost and mitigate model rollout errors, policy gradients are estimated using one-step predictions of the learned dynamics rather than multi-step propagation. This leads to an online mini-batch policy gradient framework that enables policy improvement from streamed interaction data. The proposed framework is evaluated on several simulated nonlinear control benchmarks and two real-world hardware platforms, including a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm and a Unitree Go1 quadruped. Experimental results demonstrate improved sample efficiency over model-free RL baselines, superior control performance relative to model-based RL baselines, and control performance comparable to classical model-based methods that rely on exact system dynamics.

</details>


### [168] [Strain in Sound: Soft Corrugated Tube for Local Strain Sensing with Acoustic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20017)
*Michael Chun,Ananya Nukala,Tae Myung Huh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 软波纹管传感器通过气流激发驻波共振，利用频率变化估计局部应变，机器学习算法实现高精度测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够估计软体非均匀变形的传感器，特别是用于测量局部应变，这对于软体机器人、可穿戴设备和生物医学应用具有重要意义

Method: 设计软波纹管传感器，气流通过时波纹腔体产生压力振荡激发驻波共振；拉伸管子会改变共振频率和频率-流速关系；通过控制流速扫描收集不同拉伸条件下的共振频率数据，使用梯度提升回归器进行机器学习分析

Result: 双周期波纹管（3.1 mm和4.18 mm周期）的平均绝对误差为0.8 mm，单周期波纹管（3.1 mm）的平均绝对误差为1 mm；在人体模型手指测试中能够区分多关节配置，展示了估计软体非均匀变形的潜力

Conclusion: 软波纹管传感器结合声学共振和机器学习方法，能够有效估计局部应变和非均匀变形，为软体传感提供了新思路，在软体机器人、可穿戴设备和生物医学领域具有应用前景

Abstract: We present a soft corrugated tube sensor designed to estimate strain in each half segment. When air flows through the tube, the internal corrugated cavities induce pressure oscillations that excite the tube's standing wave resonance mode, generating an acoustic tone. Stretching the tube affects both the resonance mode frequency, due to changes in overall length, and the frequency-flow speed relationship, due to variations in cavity width, which is particularly useful for local strain estimation. By sweeping flow rates in a controlled manner, we collected resonance frequency data across flow speeds under various local stretch conditions, enabling a machine learning algorithm (gradient boosting regressor) to estimate segmental strain with high accuracy. The dual-period tube design (3.1 mm and 4.18 mm corrugation periods) achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.8 mm, while the single-period tube (3.1 mm) provided a satisfactory MAE of 1 mm. Testing on a mannequin finger demonstrated the sensor's capability to differentiate multi-joint configurations, showing its potential for estimating non-uniform deformations in soft bodies.

</details>


### [169] [Toward Safe Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Interventions using World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20151)
*Harry Robertshaw,Nikola Fischer,Han-Ru Wu,Andrea Walker Perez,Weiyuan Deng,Benjamin Jackson,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TD-MPC2模型在自主机械取栓导航中表现优于SAC算法，在模拟和体外实验中均显示出更高的成功率和更安全的接触力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机械取栓面临血管几何结构高度可变和实时精确控制的挑战，现有强化学习方法在多样患者解剖结构和长导航距离下鲁棒性有限

Method: 采用基于世界模型的TD-MPC2框架，这是一种整合规划和学习动力学的模型基强化学习方法，在保留的患者特定血管结构上进行多导航任务训练，并与SAC算法进行对比验证

Result: 模拟中TD-MPC2成功率显著高于SAC（58% vs 36%），接触力远低于血管破裂阈值；体外实验中两者成功率相当，但TD-MPC2路径比更优，代价是手术时间更长

Conclusion: 这是首个在保留模拟数据和荧光引导体外实验中验证的自主机械取栓导航研究，展示了世界模型在安全、可泛化的AI辅助血管介入中的潜力

Abstract: Autonomous mechanical thrombectomy (MT) presents substantial challenges due to highly variable vascular geometries and the requirements for accurate, real-time control. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for the automation of endovascular navigation, existing approaches often show limited robustness when faced with diverse patient anatomies or extended navigation horizons. In this work, we investigate a world-model-based framework for autonomous endovascular navigation built on TD-MPC2, a model-based RL method that integrates planning and learned dynamics. We evaluate a TD-MPC2 agent trained on multiple navigation tasks across hold out patient-specific vasculatures and benchmark its performance against the state-of-the-art Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm agent. Both approaches are further validated in vitro using patient-specific vascular phantoms under fluoroscopic guidance. In simulation, TD-MPC2 demonstrates a significantly higher mean success rate than SAC (58% vs. 36%, p < 0.001), and mean tip contact forces of 0.15 N, well below the proposed 1.5 N vessel rupture threshold. Mean success rates for TD-MPC2 (68%) were comparable to SAC (60%) in vitro, but TD-MPC2 achieved superior path ratios (p = 0.017) at the cost of longer procedure times (p < 0.001). Together, these results provide the first demonstration of autonomous MT navigation validated across both hold out in silico data and fluoroscopy-guided in vitro experiments, highlighting the promise of world models for safe and generalizable AI-assisted endovascular interventions.

</details>


### [170] [Stochastic Barrier Certificates in the Presence of Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20208)
*Rayan Mazouz,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究动态障碍物环境中随机动态系统的安全性，通过随机屏障函数方法提供有限时间范围内保持在安全集合内的概率下界保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动态障碍物环境中随机动态系统的安全性问题，现有方法在处理动态障碍物时存在保守性，需要更精确的概率安全保证方法。

Method: 提出离散时间连续空间系统中时不变和时变随机屏障证书，利用Bellman最优性原理捕捉时间结构，通过多项式函数约束将时变屏障合成转化为凸的平方和规划问题。

Result: 时变屏障证书相比现有方法提供更不保守的概率下界，在非线性系统与动态障碍物的实证评估中显示紧致性保证，提高了准确性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于Bellman最优性原理的时变随机屏障函数方法能有效处理动态障碍物环境中的系统安全性，通过凸优化框架实现可计算的紧致概率安全保证。

Abstract: Safety of stochastic dynamic systems in environments with dynamic obstacles is studied in this paper through the lens of stochastic barrier functions. We introduce both time-invariant and time-varying barrier certificates for discrete-time, continuous-space systems subject to uncertainty, which provide certified lower bounds on the probability of remaining within a safe set over a finite horizon. These certificates explicitly account for time-varying unsafe regions induced by obstacle dynamics. By leveraging Bellman's optimality perspective, the time-varying formulation directly captures temporal structure and yields less conservative bounds than state-of-the-art approaches. By restricting certificates to polynomial functions, we show that time-varying barrier synthesis can be formulated as a convex sum-of-squares program, enabling tractable optimization. Empirical evaluations on nonlinear systems with dynamic obstacles show that time-varying certificates consistently achieve tight guarantees, demonstrating improved accuracy and scalability over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [171] [Toward Cooperative Driving in Mixed Traffic: An Adaptive Potential Game-Based Approach with Field Test Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20231)
*Shiyu Fang,Xiaocong Zhao,Xuekai Liu,Peng Hang,Jianqiang Wang,Yunpeng Wang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应势博弈框架解决CAV与HDV混合交通中的协同驾驶问题，通过Shapley值量化个体贡献并动态更新HDV偏好估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法忽视了个体需求和参与者异质性，难以应用于CAV与HDV共存的混合交通环境，需要能够同时优化个体和系统目标的自适应框架

Method: 提出自适应势博弈框架：1)建立基于个体效用单调关系的系统效用函数；2)引入Shapley值计算车辆的边际效用；3)通过观测HDV行为与APG估计动作的持续比较动态精炼HDV偏好估计

Result: 消融研究表明自适应更新Shapley值和HDV偏好估计显著提高了混合交通中的协同成功率；对比实验显示APG在安全性和效率方面优于其他协同方法；现场测试验证了方法在真实场景中的适用性

Conclusion: 自适应势博弈框架能够有效处理CAV与HDV混合交通中的协同驾驶问题，通过量化个体贡献和动态偏好估计实现了安全性和效率的同步提升，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs), which represent a significant advancement in autonomous driving technology, have the potential to greatly increase traffic safety and efficiency through cooperative decision-making. However, existing methods often overlook the individual needs and heterogeneity of cooperative participants, making it difficult to transfer them to environments where they coexist with human-driven vehicles (HDVs).To address this challenge, this paper proposes an adaptive potential game (APG) cooperative driving framework. First, the system utility function is established on the basis of a general form of individual utility and its monotonic relationship, allowing for the simultaneous optimization of both individual and system objectives. Second, the Shapley value is introduced to compute each vehicle's marginal utility within the system, allowing its varying impact to be quantified. Finally, the HDV preference estimation is dynamically refined by continuously comparing the observed HDV behavior with the APG's estimated actions, leading to improvements in overall system safety and efficiency. Ablation studies demonstrate that adaptively updating Shapley values and HDV preference estimation significantly improve cooperation success rates in mixed traffic. Comparative experiments further highlight the APG's advantages in terms of safety and efficiency over other cooperative methods. Moreover, the applicability of the approach to real-world scenarios was validated through field tests.

</details>


### [172] [Cortex 2.0: Grounding World Models in Real-World Industrial Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20246)
*Adriana Aida,Walida Amer,Katarina Bankovic,Dhruv Behl,Fabian Busch,Annie Bhalla,Minh Duong,Florian Gienger,Rohan Godse,Denis Grachev,Ralf Gulde,Elisa Hagensieker,Junpeng Hu,Shivam Joshi,Tobias Knoblauch,Likith Kumar,Damien LaRocque,Keerthana Lokesh,Omar Moured,Khiem Nguyen,Christian Preyss,Ranjith Sriganesan,Vikram Singh,Carsten Sponner,Anh Tong,Dominik Tuscher,Marc Tuscher,Pavan Upputuri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cortex 2.0从反应式控制转向规划-执行范式，通过在视觉潜在空间中生成候选未来轨迹并评分，显著提升工业机器人长时程操作的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人操作需要跨平台、跨任务、跨物体分布的可靠长时程执行。现有的视觉-语言-动作模型虽然表现出良好的泛化能力，但本质上是反应式的，仅基于当前观测优化下一个动作，无法评估潜在未来状态，因此在长时程任务的累积失败模式下表现脆弱。

Method: Cortex 2.0采用规划-执行范式，在视觉潜在空间中生成候选未来轨迹，对这些轨迹的预期成功率和效率进行评分，然后仅执行评分最高的候选轨迹。

Result: 在单臂和双臂操作平台上评估了四个复杂度递增的任务：拾取放置、物品和垃圾分类、螺丝分类、鞋盒拆包。Cortex 2.0在所有任务中均优于最先进的视觉-语言-动作基线方法，在具有重度杂乱、频繁遮挡和接触丰富操作的非结构化环境中保持可靠，而反应式策略在这些环境中失败。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的规划能够在复杂的工业环境中可靠运行，为工业机器人操作提供了更稳健的长时程执行解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial robotic manipulation demands reliable long-horizon execution across embodiments, tasks, and changing object distributions. While Vision-Language-Action models have demonstrated strong generalization, they remain fundamentally reactive. By optimizing the next action given the current observation without evaluating potential futures, they are brittle to the compounding failure modes of long-horizon tasks. Cortex 2.0 shifts from reactive control to plan-and-act by generating candidate future trajectories in visual latent space, scoring them for expected success and efficiency, then committing only to the highest-scoring candidate. We evaluate Cortex 2.0 on a single-arm and dual-arm manipulation platform across four tasks of increasing complexity: pick and place, item and trash sorting, screw sorting, and shoebox unpacking. Cortex 2.0 consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action baselines, achieving the best results across all tasks. The system remains reliable in unstructured environments characterized by heavy clutter, frequent occlusions, and contact-rich manipulation, where reactive policies fail. These results demonstrate that world-model-based planning can operate reliably in complex industrial environments.

</details>


### [173] [Onboard Wind Estimation for Small UAVs Equipped with Low-Cost Sensors: An Aerodynamic Model-Integrated Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20290)
*Bingchen Cheng,Tielin Ma,Jingcheng Fu,Lulu Tao,Tianhui Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用小型无人机基本传感器（无需额外风测量设备）来估计飞行状态和风的方法，通过EKF结合气动模型和自适应移动平均估计技术，提高风估计精度和平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为小型无人机实现自主风估计，以支持节能飞行，同时避免依赖昂贵的额外风测量设备，仅利用自主飞行所需的基本低成本传感器。

Method: 核心方法包括：1）扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）与气动模型集成；2）自适应移动平均估计（AMAE）技术，用于提高风估计的精度和平滑度。该方法无需流角测量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法能有效估计稳态和时变的三维风矢量。飞行测试验证了方法的有效性及实时机载计算的可行性。同时分析了气动模型精度对风估计误差的影响。

Conclusion: 该方法为小型无人机提供了可行的自主风估计方案，仅使用基本传感器即可实现，并通过系统分析不确定性和误差源为进一步改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: To enable autonomous wind estimation for energy-efficient flight in small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), this study proposes a method that estimates flight states and wind using only the low-cost essential onboard sensors required for autonomous flight, without relying on additional wind measurement devices. The core of the method includes an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with the aerodynamic model and an Adaptive Moving Average Estimation (AMAE) technique, which improves the accuracy and smoothness of the wind estimation. Simulation results show that the approach efficiently estimates both steady and time-varying 3D wind vectors without requiring flow angle measurements. The impact of aerodynamic model accuracy on wind estimation errors is also analyzed to assess practical applicability. Flight tests validate the effectiveness of the method and its feasibility for real-time onboard computation. Additionally, uncertainties and error sources encountered during testing are systematically examined, providing a foundation for further refinement.

</details>


### [174] [ETac: A Lightweight and Efficient Tactile Simulation Framework for Learning Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20295)
*Zhe Xu,Feiyu Zhao,Xiyan Huang,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ETac是一个高保真、高效率的触觉仿真框架，通过轻量级数据驱动的变形传播模型实现软体接触动力学仿真，支持大规模策略训练，在单GPU上实现4096个并行环境的强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器在灵巧机器人操作中应用日益广泛，但依赖触觉感知的学习策略面临挑战，主要源于软体仿真中保真度与计算成本之间的权衡问题。

Method: ETac采用轻量级数据驱动的变形传播模型来捕捉软体接触动力学，该框架作为仿真后端，能够产生与有限元法相当的表面变形估计，并适用于真实触觉传感器建模。

Result: 在单个RTX 4090 GPU上，ETac支持4096个并行环境的强化学习，总吞吐量达869 FPS。训练出的盲抓取策略在四种物体类型上平均成功率84.45%。

Conclusion: ETac框架展示了使基于触觉的技能学习既高效又可扩展的潜力，通过高保真、高效率的仿真解决了触觉策略学习中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are increasingly integrated into dexterous robotic manipulators to enhance contact perception. However, learning manipulation policies that rely on tactile sensing remains challenging, primarily due to the trade-off between fidelity and computational cost of soft-body simulations. To address this, we present ETac, a tactile simulation framework that models elastomeric soft-body interactions with both high fidelity and efficiency. ETac employs a lightweight data-driven deformation propagation model to capture soft-body contact dynamics, achieving high simulation quality and boosting efficiency that enables large-scale policy training. When serving as the simulation backend, ETac produces surface deformation estimates comparable to FEM and demonstrates applicability for modeling real tactile sensors. Then, we showcase its capability in training a blind grasping policy that leverages large-area tactile feedback to manipulate diverse objects. Running on a single RTX 4090 GPU, ETac supports reinforcement learning across 4,096 parallel environments, achieving a total throughput of 869 FPS. The resulting policy reaches an average success rate of 84.45% across four object types, underscoring ETac's potential to make tactile-based skill learning both efficient and scalable.

</details>


### [175] [AdaTracker: Learning Adaptive In-Context Policy for Cross-Embodiment Active Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20305)
*Kui Wu,Hao Chen,Jinzhu Han,Haijun Liu,Churan Wang,Yizhou Wang,Zhoujun Li,Si Liu,Fangwei Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaTracker：一个自适应上下文策略学习框架，通过显式建模不同机器人的物理约束和运动动力学，实现跨形态的零样本视觉目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常为每个机器人平台训练单独的模型，导致可扩展性差、泛化能力有限。不同机器人的物理约束和运动动力学差异巨大，使得构建统一的主动视觉跟踪模型具有挑战性。

Method: 提出AdaTracker框架，包含：1）Embodiment Context Encoder从历史数据推断特定机器人的约束；2）Context-Aware Policy利用上下文表示动态调制，为零样本适应推断最优控制动作；3）引入两个辅助目标确保准确的上下文识别和时间一致性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，AdaTracker在跨形态泛化、样本效率和零样本适应方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: AdaTracker通过显式建模特定形态约束并利用上下文学习，成功实现了跨不同机器人平台的统一主动视觉跟踪，具有优秀的泛化能力和适应性。

Abstract: Realizing active visual tracking with a single unified model across diverse robots is challenging, as the physical constraints and motion dynamics vary drastically from one platform to another. Existing approaches typically train separate models for each embodiment, leading to poor scalability and limited generalization. To address this, we propose AdaTracker, an adaptive in-context policy learning framework that robustly tracks targets on diverse robot morphologies. Our key insight is to explicitly model embodiment-specific constraints through an Embodiment Context Encoder, which infers embodiment-specific constraints from history. This contextual representation dynamically modulates a Context-Aware Policy, enabling it to infer optimal control actions for unseen embodiments in a zero-shot manner. To enhance robustness, we introduce two auxiliary objectives to ensure accurate context identification and temporal consistency. Experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that AdaTracker significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in cross-embodiment generalization, sample efficiency, and zero-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [176] [A Vision-Language-Action Model for Adaptive Ultrasound-Guided Needle Insertion and Needle Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20347)
*Yuelin Zhang,Qingpeng Ding,Longxiang Tang,Chengyu Fang,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于机器人超声引导下针插入的视觉-语言-动作模型，实现自适应针跟踪与插入控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导下的针插入是关键但具有挑战性的操作，现有方法依赖手工设计的流水线和模块化控制器，在复杂情况下性能下降，需要一种统一的自适应方法

Method: 提出视觉-语言-动作模型框架，包含跨深度融合跟踪头整合浅层位置和深层语义特征，跟踪条件寄存器实现参数高效的特征调节，以及不确定性感知控制策略和异步VLA流水线

Result: 在针跟踪和插入实验中均优于最先进的跟踪器和手动操作，实现了更高的跟踪精度、更高的插入成功率和更短的操作时间

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人超声智能干预提供了有前景的方向，实现了实时、动态自适应的针插入控制，提高了安全性和操作效果

Abstract: Ultrasound (US)-guided needle insertion is a critical yet challenging procedure due to dynamic imaging conditions and difficulties in needle visualization. Many methods have been proposed for automated needle insertion, but they often rely on hand-crafted pipelines with modular controllers, whose performance degrades in challenging cases. In this paper, a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model is proposed for adaptive and automated US-guided needle insertion and tracking on a robotic ultrasound (RUS) system. This framework provides a unified approach to needle tracking and needle insertion control, enabling real-time, dynamically adaptive adjustment of insertion based on the obtained needle position and environment awareness. To achieve real-time and end-to-end tracking, a Cross-Depth Fusion (CDF) tracking head is proposed, integrating shallow positional and deep semantic features from the large-scale vision backbone. To adapt the pretrained vision backbone for tracking tasks, a Tracking-Conditioning (TraCon) register is introduced for parameter-efficient feature conditioning. After needle tracking, an uncertainty-aware control policy and an asynchronous VLA pipeline are presented for adaptive needle insertion control, ensuring timely decision-making for improved safety and outcomes. Extensive experiments on both needle tracking and insertion show that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art trackers and manual operation, achieving higher tracking accuracy, improved insertion success rates, and reduced procedure time, highlighting promising directions for RUS-based intelligent intervention.

</details>


### [177] [Bimanual Robot Manipulation via Multi-Agent In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20348)
*Alessio Palma,Indro Spinelli,Vignesh Prasad,Luca Scofano,Yufeng Jin,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCICLe框架使标准LLM无需微调即可执行few-shot双手操作，通过领导者-跟随者多智能体方法解耦动作空间，在13个任务上达到71.1%成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM的上下文学习应用于双手操作面临挑战：高维关节动作空间和紧密的臂间协调约束会迅速超出标准上下文窗口容量

Method: 提出BiCICLe框架，将双手控制建模为多智能体领导者-跟随者问题，将动作空间解耦为顺序的、条件化的单臂预测；进一步扩展到"臂间辩论"迭代精炼过程，并引入第三个LLM作为"法官"评估选择最合理的协调轨迹

Result: 在TWIN基准的13个任务上，BiCICLe达到最高71.1%平均成功率，优于最佳无训练基线6.7个百分点，超越大多数监督方法；在新型任务上展示出强大的few-shot泛化能力

Conclusion: BiCICLe是首个使标准LLM无需微调即可执行few-shot双手操作的框架，通过解耦动作空间和多智能体协调方法有效解决了双手操作中的高维协调挑战

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful reasoning engines for embodied control. In particular, In-Context Learning (ICL) enables off-the-shelf, text-only LLMs to predict robot actions without any task-specific training while preserving their generalization capabilities. Applying ICL to bimanual manipulation remains challenging, as the high-dimensional joint action space and tight inter-arm coordination constraints rapidly overwhelm standard context windows. To address this, we introduce BiCICLe (Bimanual Coordinated In-Context Learning), the first framework that enables standard LLMs to perform few-shot bimanual manipulation without fine-tuning. BiCICLe frames bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem, decoupling the action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. This naturally extends to Arms' Debate, an iterative refinement process, and to the introduction of a third LLM-as-Judge to evaluate and select the most plausible coordinated trajectories. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves up to 71.1% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.7 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. We further demonstrate strong few-shot generalization on novel tasks.

</details>


### [178] [Benefits of Low-Cost Bio-Inspiration in the Age of Overparametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20365)
*Kevin Godin-Dubois,Anil Yaman,Anna V. Kononova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究比较了CPG和MLP两种生物启发式机器人控制范式在不同参数空间下的性能，发现浅层MLP和密集连接CPG在有限输入输出空间中表现更好，并引入参数影响度量证明强化学习额外参数未带来性能提升，支持进化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人控制中，CPG和MLP是广泛使用的范式，但缺乏对大规模参数空间相对优势的系统研究。当输入输出空间较小且性能受限时，更多优化参数可能阻碍而非促进学习过程，需要实证验证。

Method: 使用具有有限本体感知能力的机器人形态，在两种生物启发范式（CPG和MLP）下进行控制器优化，采用进化和强化学习两种训练协议。通过在不同奖励函数下变化参数空间，比较性能表现，并引入参数影响度量分析参数数量与性能的关系。

Result: 浅层MLP和密集连接CPG比深层MLP或Actor-Critic架构表现更好。参数影响度量显示强化学习技术所需的额外参数并未转化为更好的性能，从而支持进化策略的优势。

Conclusion: 在有限输入输出空间的机器人控制任务中，参数空间大小并非越大越好，浅层MLP和密集连接CPG是更有效的选择。进化策略相比强化学习在参数效率方面更具优势，为机器人控制器设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: While Central Pattern Generators (CPGs) and Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLP) are widely used paradigms in robot control, few systematic studies have been performed on the relative merits of large parameter spaces. In contexts where input and output spaces are small and performance is bounded, having more parameters to optimize may actively hinder the learning process instead of empowering it. To empirically measure this, we submit a given robot morphology, with limited proprioceptive capabilities, to controller optimization under two bio-inspired paradigms (CPGs and MLPs) with evolutionary- and reinforcement- trainer protocols. By varying parameter spaces across multiple reward functions, we observe that shallow MLPs and densely connected CPGs result in better performance when compared to deeper MLPs or Actor-Critic architectures. To account for the relationship between said performance and the number of parameters, we introduce a Parameter Impact metric which demonstrates that the additional parameters required by the reinforcement technique do not translate into better performance, thus favouring evolutionary strategies.

</details>


### [179] [OVPD: A Virtual-Physical Fusion Testing Dataset of OnSite Auton-omous Driving Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20423)
*Yuhang Zhang,Jiarui Zhang,Bowen Jian,Xin Zhou,Zhichao Lv,Peng Hang,Rongjie Yu,Ye Tian,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVPD是一个虚拟物理融合的自动驾驶测试数据集，基于2025年OnSite自动驾驶挑战赛，通过真实车辆在环测试，整合虚拟背景交通和车路协同感知，构建可控的闭环测试环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集缺乏真实车辆动力学反馈以及与周围交通和道路基础设施的闭环交互，难以反映自动驾驶系统的实际部署准备度。

Method: 采用真实车辆在环测试方法，在试验场环境中整合虚拟背景交通与车路协同感知，构建可控的交互式闭环测试环境。数据集包含20个测试片段，覆盖15个原子场景链，总计近3小时多模态数据。

Result: OVPD数据集支持长尾规划和决策验证、开环或平台支持的闭环评估，以及安全、效率、舒适性、规则遵守和交通影响等多维度综合评估，为故障诊断和迭代改进提供可操作证据。

Conclusion: OVPD填补了现有自动驾驶测试数据集的空白，通过虚拟物理融合方法提供了高保真、可重放、可诊断的测试数据，支持全面的自动驾驶系统评估和改进。

Abstract: The rapid iteration of autonomous driving algorithms has created a growing demand for high-fidelity, replayable, and diagnosable testing data. However, many public datasets lack real vehicle dynamics feedback and closed-loop interaction with surrounding traffic and road infrastructure, limiting their ability to reflect deployment readiness. To address this gap, we present OVPD (OnSite Virtual-Physical Dataset), a virtual-physical fusion testing dataset released from the 2025 OnSite Autonomous Driving Challenge. Centered on real-vehicle-in-the-loop testing, OVPD integrates virtual background traffic with vehicle-infrastructure perception to build controllable and interactive closed-loop test environments on a proving ground. The dataset contains 20 testing clips from 20 teams over a scenario chain of 15 atomic scenarios, totaling nearly 3 hours of multi-modal data, including vehicle trajectories and states, control commands, and digital-twin-rendered surround-view observations. OVPD supports long-tail planning and decision-making validation, open-loop or platform-enabled closed-loop evaluation, and comprehensive assessment across safety, efficiency, comfort, rule compliance, and traffic impact, providing actionable evidence for failure diagnosis and iterative improvement. The dataset is available via: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Yuhang253820/Onsite_OPVD

</details>


### [180] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将多目标词典优化转化为单目标标量优化，通过非均匀量化和位移操作，结合改进的MPPI求解器和新的谓词鲁棒性度量，实现高效的最小违反STL运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划需要满足多个可能冲突的规范，当无法同时满足所有规范时，需要根据优先级最小化规范违反。STL提供了形式化语言来定义规范，但词典优化问题计算成本高，需要更高效的求解方法

Method: 1. 使用非均匀量化和位移操作将多目标词典优化转化为单目标标量优化问题；2. 扩展确定性模型预测路径积分(MPPI)求解器，支持无二次输入成本的优化；3. 提出结合空间和时间违反的新谓词鲁棒性度量

Result: 所提方法在单目标求解器框架内为词典STL最小违反运动规划提供了可解释且可扩展的解决方案，能够高效处理多规范冲突情况

Conclusion: 通过将词典优化转化为单目标优化，结合改进的MPPI求解器和新的鲁棒性度量，实现了高效、可解释的STL最小违反运动规划，为解决自动驾驶中多规范冲突问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [181] [VTouch++: A Multimodal Dataset with Vision-Based Tactile Enhancement for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20444)
*Qianxi Hua,Xinyue Li,Zheng Yan,Yang Li,Chi Zhang,Yongyao Li,Yufei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTOUCH数据集：基于视觉触觉感知的双手机器人操作数据集，提供高保真物理交互信号，采用矩阵式任务设计，并通过自动化数据收集实现可扩展性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作（特别是接触丰富的任务）仍然具有挑战性，主要原因是缺乏具有丰富物理交互信号、系统任务组织和足够规模的数据集

Method: 1. 利用基于视觉的触觉感知提供高保真物理交互信号；2. 采用矩阵式任务设计实现系统化学习；3. 部署自动化数据收集管道覆盖现实世界需求驱动场景确保可扩展性

Result: 1. 通过跨模态检索和真实机器人评估进行了广泛的定量实验验证数据集有效性；2. 展示了在多个机器人、策略和任务上的泛化推理能力，证明了现实世界性能

Conclusion: VTOUCH数据集解决了双手机器人操作中的关键数据限制问题，为接触丰富的操作任务提供了系统化、可扩展的数据解决方案，并通过实验验证了其有效性和泛化能力

Abstract: Embodied intelligence has advanced rapidly in recent years; however, bimanual manipulation-especially in contact-rich tasks remains challenging. This is largely due to the lack of datasets with rich physical interaction signals, systematic task organization, and sufficient scale. To address these limitations, we introduce the VTOUCH dataset. It leverages vision based tactile sensing to provide high-fidelity physical interaction signals, adopts a matrix-style task design to enable systematic learning, and employs automated data collection pipelines covering real-world, demand-driven scenarios to ensure scalability. To further validate the effectiveness of the dataset, we conduct extensive quantitative experiments on cross-modal retrieval as well as real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate real-world performance through generalizable inference across multiple robots, policies, and tasks.

</details>


### [182] [MOMO: A framework for seamless physical, verbal, and graphical robot skill learning and adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20468)
*Markus Knauer,Edoardo Fiorini,Maximilian Mühlbauer,Stefan Schneyer,Promwat Angsuratanawech,Florian Samuel Lay,Timo Bachmann,Samuel Bustamante,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério,Thomas Eiband*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态机器人技能适应框架，集成触觉、自然语言和图形界面三种交互方式，用于工业机器人任务的灵活调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人应用需要非专家用户能够轻松适应不同任务和环境的灵活系统，但不同适应需求需要不同的交互方式。

Method: 集成五种组件：基于能量的人类意图检测、基于工具的LLM架构（选择预定义函数而非生成代码）、核化运动基元（KMPs）进行运动编码、概率虚拟夹具用于引导演示记录、遍历控制用于表面精加工。

Result: 基于工具的LLM架构能够将技能适应从KMPs推广到遍历控制，实现语音命令的表面精加工。在Automatica 2025展会上使用7自由度扭矩控制机器人验证了工业环境中的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该多模态交互框架为工业机器人技能适应提供了灵活、用户友好的解决方案，通过三种互补的交互方式满足不同适应需求，在工业环境中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Industrial robot applications require increasingly flexible systems that non-expert users can easily adapt for varying tasks and environments. However, different adaptations benefit from different interaction modalities. We present an interactive framework that enables robot skill adaptation through three complementary modalities: kinesthetic touch for precise spatial corrections, natural language for high-level semantic modifications, and a graphical web interface for visualizing geometric relations and trajectories, inspecting and adjusting parameters, and editing via-points by drag-and-drop. The framework integrates five components: energy-based human-intention detection, a tool-based LLM architecture (where the LLM selects and parameterizes predefined functions rather than generating code) for safe natural language adaptation, Kernelized Movement Primitives (KMPs) for motion encoding, probabilistic Virtual Fixtures for guided demonstration recording, and ergodic control for surface finishing. We demonstrate that this tool-based LLM architecture generalizes skill adaptation from KMPs to ergodic control, enabling voice-commanded surface finishing. Validation on a 7-DoF torque-controlled robot at the Automatica 2025 trade fair demonstrates the practical applicability of our approach in industrial settings.

</details>


### [183] [Temporal Difference Calibration in Sequential Tasks: Application to Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20472)
*Shelly Francis-Meretzki,Mirco Mutti,Yaniv Romano,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于机器人视觉-语言-动作模型的序列校准方法，将不确定性校准与强化学习中的价值函数联系起来，通过时序差分学习改进序列任务中的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉-语言-动作模型在序列任务中需要可靠的不确定性量化，但现有方法在部分轨迹观测下的校准评估和改进仍未被充分探索，特别是在任务成功仅在结束时确定的情况下。

Method: 提出了序列校准的公式化方法，引入了Brier分数的序列扩展，证明了对于二元结果，其风险最小化器与VLA策略的价值函数一致，从而将不确定性校准与强化学习联系起来，使用时序差分价值估计作为时间上的原则性校准机制。

Result: 实验表明，时序差分校准在模拟和真实机器人数据上相对于最先进方法提高了性能。有趣的是，当使用时序差分校准时，VLA的单步动作概率可以产生有竞争力的不确定性估计，这与之前使用不同校准技术的研究发现形成对比。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了不确定性校准与强化学习之间的理论联系，通过时序差分价值估计为序列任务提供了有效的校准机制，显著改进了视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人任务中的不确定性量化性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models for robotics have highlighted the importance of reliable uncertainty quantification in sequential tasks. However, assessing and improving calibration in such settings remains mostly unexplored, especially when only partial trajectories are observed. In this work, we formulate sequential calibration for episodic tasks, where task-success confidence is produced along an episode, while success is determined at the end of it. We introduce a sequential extension of the Brier score and show that, for binary outcomes, its risk minimizer coincides with the VLA policy's value function. This connection bridges uncertainty calibration and reinforcement learning, enabling the use of temporal-difference (TD) value estimation as a principled calibration mechanism over time. We empirically show that TD calibration improves performance relative to the state-of-the-art on simulated and real-robot data. Interestingly, we show that when calibrated using TD, the VLA's single-step action probabilities can yield competitive uncertainty estimates, in contrast to recent findings that employed different calibration techniques.

</details>


### [184] [Passive Variable Impedance For Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20557)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Nepomuk Werner,Ribin Balachandran,Thomas Hulin,João Silvério,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种稳定可变刚度阻抗控制和多控制器仲裁的完整框架，识别并解决闭环系统中的无源性违反问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享控制中，阻抗控制用于跟踪目标位姿，其引导行为由刚度增益决定。当同时跟踪多个重要性不同的目标位姿时，需要随时间变化的权重来仲裁相应的输出力矩。现有方法在可变刚度和控制器仲裁方面缺乏稳定性保证

Method: 将可变刚度阻抗控制和控制器仲裁（通过输出力矩的缩放加法）重新表述为统一框架，识别闭环系统中的无源性违反，并提供使系统无源化的方法。不限制刚度矩阵或仲裁因子的设计，两者都可以是包含非对角元素的矩阵值，并随时间任意变化

Result: 提出的方法能够稳定标准阻抗控制器，允许开发新颖灵活的共享控制方法。在仿真和不同系统的真实机器人实验中验证了方法的有效性，展示了可根据共享控制需求利用的不同行为

Conclusion: 建立了一个完整的框架来稳定可变刚度阻抗控制和多控制器仲裁，解决了无源性违反问题，为开发更灵活的共享控制方法提供了理论基础和实用工具

Abstract: Shared Control methods often use impedance control to track target poses in a robotic manipulator. The guidance behavior of such controllers is shaped by the used stiffness gains, which can be varying over time to achieve an adaptive guiding. When multiple target poses are tracked at the same time with varying importance, the corresponding output wrenches have to be arbitrated with weightings changing over time. In this work, we study the stabilization of both variable stiffness in impedance control as well as the arbitration of different controllers through a scaled addition of their output wrenches, reformulating both into a holistic framework. We identify passivity violations in the closed loop system and provide methods to passivate the system. The resulting approach can be used to stabilize standard impedance controllers, allowing for the development of novel and flexible shared control methods. We do not constrain the design of stiffness matrices or arbitration factors; both can be matrix-valued including off-diagonal elements and change arbitrarily over time. The proposed methods are furthermore validated in simulation as well as in real robot experiments on different systems, proving their effectiveness and showcasing different behaviors which can be utilized depending on the requirements of the shared control approach.

</details>


### [185] [Kinematic Optimization of Phalanx Length Ratios in Robotic Hands Using Potential Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20686)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Jeongdo Ahn,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于潜在灵巧度的五指机器人手指指骨长度比优化框架，使用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度作为评估指标，通过加权目标函数在约束下识别最优设计配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人手设计阶段，如果不定义特定物体或操作任务，很难定量评估指骨长度比对灵巧度的影响。因此需要一种系统化的框架来优化指骨长度比，基于运动学结构中的潜在灵巧度进行设计。

Method: 采用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度作为评估指标，使用加权目标函数在约束条件下优化设计。工作空间采用体素表示进行离散化，关节运动以均匀间隔离散化进行评估。对拇指和其他手指的设计集进行优化，排除不产生重叠工作空间的设计组合。

Result: 结果显示每个指骨对整体灵巧度的贡献并不相等，识别了影响每个指骨的因素。此外，由于设计空间中评估指标的非均匀分布，权重系数的选择不一定直接最大化单个性能指标。框架能够系统分析可达性、灵巧度和可控性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为多指机器人手的运动学设计提供了系统化的方法，可作为实际设计指南，分析可达性、灵巧度和可控性之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: In the design stage of robotic hands, it is not straightforward to quantitatively evaluate the effect of phalanx length ratios on dexterity without defining specific objects or manipulation tasks. Therefore, this study presents a framework for optimizing the phalanx length ratios of a five-finger robotic hand based on potential dexterity within a kinematic structure. The proposed method employs global manipulability, workspace volume, overlap workspace volume, and fingertip sensitivity as evaluation metrics, and identifies optimal design configurations using a weighted objective function under given constraints. The reachable workspace is discretized using a voxel-based representation, and joint motions are discretized at uniform intervals for evaluation. The optimization is performed over design sets for both the thumb and the other fingers, and design combinations that do not generate overlap workspace are excluded. The results show that each phalanx does not contribute equally to the overall dexterity, and the factors influencing each phalanx are identified. In addition, it is observed that the selection of weighting coefficients does not necessarily lead to the direct maximization of individual performance metrics, due to the non-uniform distribution of evaluation measures within the design space. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to analyze the trade-offs among reachability, dexterity, and controllability, and can serve as a practical guideline for the kinematic design of multi-fingered robotic hands.

</details>


### [186] [A Kinematic Framework for Evaluating Pinch Configurations in Robotic Hand Design without Object or Contact Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20692)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Hyunmok Jung,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于指尖工作空间交互的机器人手捏取能力运动学评估方法，无需物体几何或接触力模型信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取评估方法依赖物体几何或接触力模型，限制了在机器人手设计早期阶段的应用，需要一种仅基于运动学结构的捏取能力评估方法

Method: 计算每个指尖的可达工作空间，通过评估指尖对之间的关系检测可行的捏取配置，基于机器人手的运动学结构进行分析

Result: 该方法能够仅基于运动学结构评估机器人手的捏取能力，分析了四种不同运动学结构对手捏取配置的影响

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架可作为比较不同机器人手设计、在设计阶段分析捏取能力的有用工具

Abstract: Evaluating the pinch capability of a robotic hand is important for understanding its functional dexterity. However, many existing grasp evaluation methods rely on object geometry or contact force models, which limits their applicability during the early stages of robotic hand design. This study proposes a kinematic evaluation method for analyzing pinch configurations of robotic hands based on interactions between fingertip workspaces. First, the reachable workspace of each fingertip is computed from the joint configurations of the fingers. Then, feasible pinch configurations are detected by evaluating the relationships between fingertip pairs. Since the proposed method does not require information about object geometry or contact force models, the pinch capability of a robotic hand can be evaluated solely based on its kinematic structure. In addition, analyses are performed on four different kinematic structures of the hand to investigate their impact on the pinch configurations. The proposed evaluation framework can serve as a useful tool for comparing different robotic hand designs and analyzing pinch capability during the design stage.

</details>


### [187] [Visual-Tactile Peg-in-Hole Assembly Learning from Peg-out-of-Hole Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20712)
*Yongqiang Zhao,Xuyang Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Matteo Leonetti,Emmanouil Spyrakos-Papastavridis,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用逆向任务（peg-out-of-hole）来促进正向任务（peg-in-hole）学习的视觉-触觉技能学习框架，通过反向和随机化逆向任务轨迹为正向任务提供专家数据，显著提高了装配成功率和降低了接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peg-in-hole装配是基础但具有挑战性的机器人操作任务。虽然强化学习在该任务上显示出潜力，但需要大量探索。逆向任务（peg-out-of-hole）本质上更容易，因为它只需要克服现有摩擦力而无需精确对齐，使数据收集更高效。

Method: 将PooH和PiH都建模为具有共享视觉-触觉观测空间的POMDP。首先训练视觉-触觉PooH策略；将其轨迹（包含运动学、视觉和触觉信息）进行时间反转和动作随机化，为PiH提供专家数据。在策略学习中，视觉感知促进钉孔接近，而触觉测量补偿钉孔错位。
